Board. Prospective investors should be aware of the potential            經過審慎周詳考慮後方作出投資決定。
risks of investing in such companies and should make the
decision to invest only after due and careful consideration.

and mid-sized companies, there is a risk that securities traded          GEM 買賣的證券可能會較於主板買賣的證券承
on GEM may be more susceptible to high market volatility than            受較大的市場波動風險，同時無法保證在 GEM
securities traded on the Main Board and no assurance is given            買賣的證券會有高流通量的市場。
that there will be a liquid market in the securities traded on
GEM.

Dear Shareholders,                                                       各位股東：

On behalf of Chong Fai Jewellery Group Holdings Company Limited          代表創輝珠寶集團控股有限公司（「本公司」，連
(the “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”), I          同其附屬公司，統稱為「本集團」），謹此提呈本
hereby present the Group’s annual report for the year ended 31           集團截至 2026 年 3 月 31 日止年度的年度報告。
March 2026.

The Group is a vertically integrated jeweller based in Hong Kong         本集團是建基於香港的垂直整合珠寶商，主要從
and is primarily engaged in the retail of its own products through its   事透過由七間位於九龍及新界策略地點的「創輝
network of seven retail stores under the brand name of “Chong Fai        珠寶」品牌零售店所組成的網絡，零售其自家產
Jewellery（創輝珠寶）” strategically located in Kowloon and the New            品。
Territories.

The Group’s principal products are jewellery products including          本集團的主要產品是珠寶產品，包括寶石鑲嵌珠
gem-set jewellery and gold jewellery products. The Group also            寶及黃金珠寶產品。本集團亦從事向其他珠寶零
engages in the wholesale of its jewellery products to other jewellery    售商批發其珠寶產品，並售賣從公眾回收的黃金
retailers, and sale of gold products which are recycled from the         產品。
general public.

Looking forward, the Group will seek to expand the Group’s               展望未來，當本港經濟環境及零售業進一步好
jewellery design collection in line with customer needs, preferences     轉，本集團將致力擴大本集團的珠寶設計系列，
and market trends and expand its retail presence to capture a wider      以迎合客戶需求、喜好及市場趨勢，同時亦會擴
range of market opportunities when the economic environment and          充其零售據點，把握各種市場機遇。我們正將市
retail industry in Hong Kong further improve. We are extending our       場推廣活動拓展至社交媒體，以為本公司股東帶
marketing campaign to social media in the hope that it will bring a      來豐厚回報，並維持本集團的長遠發展。
desirable return to the shareholders of the Company and sustain a
long-term growth of the Group.

    The Group is a vertically integrated jeweller based in Hong Kong         本集團是建基於香港的垂直整合珠寶商，主要從

    Fai Jewellery（創輝珠寶）” strategically located in Kowloon and the            品。垂直整合的結構使本集團有辦法控制其自行
    New Territories. Being vertically integrated, the Group is capable       創作的自家珠寶設計的多樣化、品質及定價。
    of controlling the diversity, quality and pricing of its own jewellery
    designs products.

    gem-set jewellery and gold jewellery products. The Group also            寶及黃金珠寶產品。本集團亦從事向其他珠寶零
    engages in the wholesale of its jewellery products to other jewellery    售商批發其珠寶產品，並售賣從公眾回收的黃金

    general public.

     OUTLOOK                                                              前景
     During the year ended 31 March 2026, the Hong Kong retail            截至 2026 年 3 月 31 日止年度，香港零售市場顯
     market staged a notable recovery, buoyed by renewed consumer         著回暖，受惠於消費信心復甦及金價持續上揚。
     confidence and a sustained surge in gold prices. The Group           本集團充分利用此利好形勢，金飾相關產品需求
     fully capitalised on this favourable shift, with strong demand for   殷切，帶動總收益顯著增長。憑藉對市場的深刻
     gold related products driving a marked increase in total revenue.    洞察及靈活調配營運資源，集團較截至 2025 年
     Leveraging its deep market insight and agile resource allocation,    3 月 31 日止年度錄得大幅提升的盈利。
     the Group achieved significantly higher profits compared with the
     year ended 31 March 2025.

     Looking forward, once the economic environment of Hong Kong          展望未來，當本港經濟環境好改善，本集團將致
     improves, the Group will seek to expand the Group’s jewellery        力擴大本集團的珠寶設計系列，以迎合客戶需

     customers and market trends, and expand its retail presence to       點，把握各種市場機遇。我們正將市場推廣活動
     capture a wider range of market opportunities. We are extending      拓展至社交媒體，期望為股東帶來豐厚回報，並
     our marketing campaign to the social media in hope that it will      維持本集團的長遠發展。

Internal Control System                                                    內部控制系统

The Company has maintained an internal control system and its              本公司已維持內部控制系統，且其實施已獲審核
implementation has been considered by the Audit Committee and              委員會及董事會視為有效。同時，本集團致力透
the Board as effective. Meanwhile, the Group strives for continual         過努力加強控制、提供持續僱員培訓及發展、挽
improvement through efforts to enhance controls by providing               留人才及其他措施作出持續改進。本集團憑藉強
ongoing employee training and development, talent retention, and           大內部管治及獨立董事會監督遵循嚴格及平衡的
other measures. The Group follows a disciplined and balanced               薪酬框架。本集團在進行表現評估及激勵薪酬程
compensation framework with strong internal governance and                 序時會仔細考慮風險及控制問題的影響。
independent Board oversight. The impact of risk and control issues
are carefully considered in the Group’s performance evaluation and

     INFORMATION TECHNOLOGY                                                  資訊科技
     The Group has one main IT platform that supports its business           本集團設有一個支援其經營業務的主要資訊科技
     operations, namely POS system. The IT platform was developed            平台，即 POS 系統。為配合本集團的需要，資
     by an independent third party contractor to suit the Group’s            訊科技平台由獨立第三方承包商開發，並會持續
     requirements and it is continually refined and upgraded to meet the     改善及升級以符合本集團的業務需求。
     Group’s business needs.

     The Group’s POS system records sales and inventory movements as         本集團的 POS 系統連接每間零售店，並記錄本
     it is linked to every retail store. The Group’s management is able to   集團的銷售及庫存變動。本集團的管理層能通過
     manage effectively the retail stores through the POS system which       POS 系統有效管理零售店，從而為其提供每間
     provides them with the performance of each retail store and its         零售店的表現及庫存狀況。本集團 POS 系統追
     inventory status. With the ability of the Group’s POS system to track   蹤各零售店存貨變動的能力，讓董事更了解每間
     the inventory movements in each retail store, the Directors are in a    零售店受歡迎產品的類別，從而令本集團得以更
     better position to assess what type of products are more popular in     迅速回應市場需求。
     each retail store, which in turn enables the Group to respond more
     swiftly to market demands.

SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                        股份獎勵計劃
The Company adopted the Share Award Scheme on 19 March 2021.              本公司於 2021 年 3 月 19 日採納一項股份獎勵計
It is a share incentive scheme which is established to recognise          劃。該計劃為一項股份獎勵計劃，乃為嘉許及肯
and acknowledge the contributions that the eligible persons have          定合資格參與者對本集團所作出或可能作出的貢
had or may have made to the Group. The terms of the Share Award           獻而設立。股份獎勵計劃的條款符合 GEM 上市

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CODE                                             企業管治守則
In the opinion of the Directors, except for the deviation from code   董事認為，於最後實際可行日期，除偏離企業
provision C.2.1 in Part 2 of the Corporate Governance Code (“CG       管 治 守 則（「企 業 管 治 守 則 」）條 文 第 2 部 份 的 第
Code”), the Group has no material deviation from Part 2 of the        C.2.1 條外，於本年報所涵蓋的整個會計期間，
CG Code as set forth in Appendix C1 to the GEM Listing Rules          本集團並無重大偏離 GEM 上市規則附錄 C1 所載
throughout the accounting period covered by this annual report.       的企業管治守則第 2 部份。企業管治守則條文第
Code provision C.2.1 in Part 2 of the CG Code stipulates that the     2 部份的第 C.2.1 條訂明，主席與行政總裁的角
roles of chairman and chief executive should be separate and          色應有區分，不應由一人同時兼任。傅先生為本
should not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Fu is both        公司主席兼行政總裁。有鑒於傅先生一直承擔本
the Chairman and the Chief Executive Officer of the Company. In       集團日常營運及管理的職責，董事會認為，憑藉
view that Mr. Fu has been assuming day-to-day responsibilities in     傅先生對本集團業務的豐富經驗及知識，傅先生
operating and managing the Group, the Board believes that with        同時授任本公司主席及行政總裁職位，可為本公
the support of Mr. Fu’s extensive experience and knowledge in the     司提供更穩固及一致的領導，從而作出更有效業
business of the Group, vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief   務規劃及決策，乃符合本集團最佳利益。
Executive Officer of the Company in Mr. Fu strengthens the solid
and consistent leadership and thereby allows for efficient business
planning and decision which is in the best interest of the Group.




                                                                                      2025/26 ANNUAL REPORT 年報   55
     REPORT OF DIRECTORS
     董事會報告



     The Directors consider that the deviation from code provision C.2.1   董事認為，在上述情況下，偏離企業管治守則條
     in Part 2 of the CG Code is appropriate in such circumstances.        文第 2 部份的第 C.2.1 條乃為適宜。儘管出現上
     Notwithstanding the above, the Board is of the view that this         文所述情況，董事會認為，此管理架構對本集團
     management structure is effective for the Group’s operations, and     營業有效，且已實施充足制衡措施。
     sufficient checks and balances are in place.

     The ESG Governance Structure                                             環境、社會及管治治理結構

     The Group has established the ESG Taskforce (the “Taskforce”).           本集團成立了環境、社會及管治工作小組（「工作
     The Taskforce comprises core members from different departments          小組」）。工作小組由本集團不同部門的核心成員
     and is responsible for collecting relevant information on our ESG        組成，負責搜集我們在環境、社會及管治方面的
     aspects for preparing the ESG Report. The Taskforce reports              相關資料及資料以編製本報告。工作小組會定期
     to the Board of Directors (the “Board”), assists in identifying          向董事會彙報，協助辨識和評估本集團的環境、
     and evaluating the Group’s ESG risks and the effectiveness of            社會及管治風險以及評估本集團內部控制機制的
     the internal control mechanisms. The Taskforce also examines             有效性。工作小組亦會檢查和評估我們在環境、
     and evaluates our performances in different aspects such as              社會及管治範疇內環境、安全生產、勞工標準、
     environment, health and safety, labour standards and product             產品責任等不同方面的表現。董事會則會設定本
     responsibilities in the ESG aspects. The Board sets up a general         集團環境、社會及管治戰略上的大方向，並確保
     direction for the Group’s ESG strategies, ensuring the effectiveness     其風險控制及內部控制機制的有效性。
     in the control of its risks and internal control mechanisms.

     Paper Management                                               紙張管理

     Office                                                         辦公室

     One of the main natural resources consumed by the              本集團主要自然資源消耗之一是辦
     Group is paper from its office printing machines. To           公 室 印 刷 機 用 紙。 為 盡 量 減 少 紙 張
     minimise the use of paper, the Group has put great             的 使 用， 本 集 團 已 大 力 推 行 以 下 措
     efforts into the implementation of following initiatives:      施：

     •        Chosen suppliers with more environmentally            •    選擇更環保的紙張來源供應
              friendly paper source, so as to indirectly reduce          商，以便在消耗相同數量紙張
              the amount of tree losses while consuming the              的同時間接減少樹木損失量；
              same amount of paper;

     •        Promoted paperless office, and disseminate            •    推廣無紙化辦公室，並儘可能
              information by electronic means (i.e. via email or         通 過 電 子 方 式（如 通 過 電 子 郵
              e-bulletin boards) as much as possible;                    件或電子公告板）傳播信息；

     •        Set duplex printing as the default mode for most      •    當需要打印時，將雙面打印設
              network printers when printouts are needed;                置為大多數網絡打印機的默認
                                                                         模式；

     •        Disseminated the idea of “Think before print” by      •    通過在辦公室使用海報和貼紙
              using posters and stickers in offices to remind the        傳播「影印前先思考」的想法，
              staff of avoiding unnecessary printings;                   提醒員工避免不必要的影印；

     •        Reconsidered boxes and trays as containers            •    重新考慮箱子和托盤作為複印
              beside photocopier to collect single-sided paper           機 旁 邊 的 容 器， 收 集 單 面 紙
              for reuse and recycling;                                   張，以便再利用和回收 ;

     •        Used the back of old single-sided documents for       •    使用舊的單面文檔背面進行打
              printing or as draft paper;                                印或作為草稿紙；

     •        Recycled used stationery whenever possible; and       •    盡可能使用回收的舊文具；及

     •        Used as less as paper whenever possible.              •    盡可能少用紙。

     the implementation of following measures:                      以下措施：

     •        Tried to minimise printing excessive sales receipts   •    嘗試在客戶購買時盡量減少編

      AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT OF                              核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責任
      THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether          吾等的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在
      the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from           由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理
      material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue       保證，並按照吾等所協定的委聘條款僅向全體股
      an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion     東出具包括吾等意見的核數師報告，除此以外，
      solely to you, as a body, in accordance with our agreed terms            吾等的報告不可用作其他用途。吾等並不就本報
      of engagements and for no other purpose. We do not assume                告之內容對任何其他人士負上或承擔責任。
      responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the
      contents of this report.

      Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a          合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香港
      guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will          審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在
      always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements      時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，
      can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,            如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合
      individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected      財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟
      to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of       決定，則有關的錯誤陳述被視作重大。
      these consolidated financial statements.

      As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise                在根據香港審計準則進行審計的過程中，吾等運
      professional judgment and maintain professional skepticism               用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。吾等亦：
      throughout the audit. We also:

      •     identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the      •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合財
            consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or             務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及
            error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to               執行審計程序以應對這些風險，以及獲取
            those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and          充足和適當的審計憑證，作為吾等意見的
            appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of            基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄
            not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is          意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之
            higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve         上，因此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯

1.   GENERAL INFORMATION                                              1.   一般資料
     Chong Fai Jewellery Group Holdings Company Limited (the               創 輝 珠 寶 集 團 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司 」）

     POLICIES – continued
     3.2   Basis of consolidation                                               3.2   綜合基準

           The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                      綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬
           financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries                   公 司 所 編 製 截 至 每 年 3 月 31 日 的 財
           made up to 31 March each year.                                             務報表。

           Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                     附 屬 公 司 指 受 本 集 團 控 制 的 實 體。
           Group controls an entity when the Group is exposed,                        當本集團承受或享有參與實體所得
           or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement                    的 可 變 回 報， 且 有 能 力 透 過 其 對 實
           with the entity and has the ability to affect those returns                體 的 權 力 影 響 該 等 回 報 時， 則 其 控
           through its power over the entity. When assessing                          制 該 實 體。 評 估 本 集 團 是 否 對 實 體
           whether the Group has power over the entity, only                          有 權 力 時， 僅 考 慮 與 實 體 有 關 的 實
           substantive rights relating to the entity (held by the                     質權利（由本集團及其他持有）。
           Group and others) are considered.

           The Group includes the income and expenses of a                            本集團自其取得控制權當日至本集
           subsidiary in the consolidated financial statements from                   團 不 再 控 制 附 屬 公 司 當 日， 在 綜 合
           the date it gains control until the date when the Group                    財務報表內載列該附屬公司的收入
           ceases to control the subsidiary.                                          及開支。

           Intra-group transactions, balances and unrealised gains                    集 團 間 交 易、 結 餘 及 集 團 公 司 間 交
           and losses on transactions between group companies                         易的未變現收益及虧損於編製綜合
           are eliminated in preparing the consolidated financial                     財 務 報 表 時 對 銷。 倘 集 團 間 資 產 銷
           statements. Where unrealised losses on sales of                            售的未變現虧損於綜合入賬時撥
           intra-group asset are reversed on consolidation, the                       回， 則 亦 會 從 本 集 團 角 度 就 有 關 資
           underlying asset is also tested for impairment from the                    產 進 行 減 值 測 試。 於 附 屬 公 司 財 務
           Group’s perspective. Amounts reported in the financial                     報表所呈報的款項已進行調整（倘必
           statements of subsidiaries have been adjusted where                        要）以確保與本集團所採納會計政策

           3.4   Foreign currency translation                                      3.4   外幣換算

                 The consolidated financial statements are presented in                  綜 合 財 務 報 表 乃 以 港 元 呈 列， 與 本
                 Hong Kong Dollars (HK$), which is also the functional                   公司之功能貨幣一致。
                 currency of the Company.

                 In the individual financial statements of the consolidated              就經綜合實體各自之財務報表而
                 entities, foreign currency transactions are translated                  言， 外 幣 交 易 按 交 易 當 日 適 用 之 匯
                 into the functional currency of the individual entity                   率 換 算 為 該 個 別 實 體 之 功 能 貨 幣。
                 using the exchange rates prevailing at the dates of                     於 報 告 期 末， 以 外 幣 計 值 之 貨 幣 資
                 the transactions. At the end of the reporting period,                   產 及 負 債 按 該 日 適 用 之 匯 率 換 算。
                 monetary assets and liabilities denominated in foreign                  因結算有關交易及於報告期末重新
                 currencies are translated at the foreign exchange rates                 換算貨幣資產及負債而產生之匯兌
                 ruling at that date. Foreign exchange gains and losses                  收益及虧損於損益內確認。
                 resulting from the settlement of such transactions and
                 from the end of the reporting period retranslation of
                 monetary assets and liabilities are recognised in profit
                 or loss.

                 Non-monetary items carried at fair value that are                       以外幣計值及按公平值列賬之非貨
                 denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at                   幣項目按釐定公平值當日適用之匯
                 the rates prevailing on the date when the fair value was                率 重 新 換 算。 以 外 幣 計 值 及 按 歷 史
                 determined. Non-monetary items that are measured in                     成本計量之非貨幣項目不會進行換
                 terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not                  算（即僅按交易日期之匯率換算）。
                 retranslated (i.e. only translated using the exchange
                 rates at the transaction date).

                 In the consolidated financial statements, all individual                於 綜 合 財 務 報 表 內， 所 有 境 外 業 務
                 financial statements of foreign operations, originally                  各自先前以本集團呈報貨幣以外之
                 presented in a currency different from the Group’s                      貨 幣 列 值 之 財 務 報 表， 均 已 換 算 為
                 presentation currency, have been converted into Hong                    港 元。 資 產 及 負 債 按 報 告 期 末 之 收
                 Kong dollars. Assets and liabilities have been translated               市 匯 率 換 算 為 港 元， 而 收 入 及 支 出
                 into Hong Kong dollars at the closing rates at the end                  則按交易日期之適用匯率或按報告
                 of the reporting period. Income and expenses have                       期間之平均匯率（倘匯率並無大幅上
                 been converted into the Hong Kong dollars at the                        落）換算為港元。此程序產生之任何
                 exchange rates ruling at the transaction dates, or at                   差 額 已 確 認 為 其 他 全 面 收 益， 並 於
                 the average rates over the reporting period provided                    權益中之外匯兌儲備內獨立累計。
                 that the exchange rates do not fluctuate significantly.
                 Any differences arising from this procedure have
                 been recognised in other comprehensive income and
                 accumulated separately in the exchange reserve in
                 equity.

           3.7   Impairment of financial assets                                     3.7   金融資產減值

                 The impairment requirements of HKFRS 9 “Financial                        香港財務報告準則第 9 號「金融工具」
                 instruments” (“HKFRS 9”) use forward-looking                             （「香港財務報告準則第 9 號」）的減值
                 information to recognise ECL – the “ECL model”.                          規定採用前瞻性的資料確認預期信
                 Instruments within the scope included loans and other                    貸虧損 – 「預期信貸虧損模式」。屬
                 debt-type financial assets measured at amortised cost                    此規定範疇內的工具包括按攤銷成
                 and trade receivables.                                                   本計量的貸款及其他債券類金融資
                                                                                          產及貿易應收款項。

                 The Group considers a broader range of information                       本集團在評估信用風險和計量預期
                 when assessing credit risk and measuring ECL,                            信 用 損 失 時 考 慮 更 廣 泛 的 信 息， 包
                 including historical credit loss experience adjusted                     括根據債務人特定因素調整的歷史
                 for factors that are specific to the debtors, general                    信 用 損 失 經 驗、 總 體 經 濟 狀 況 以 及
                 economic conditions and an assessment of both the                        對報告期末當前狀況的評估以及對
                 current conditions at the end of the reporting period as                 未來情況的預測。
                 well as the forecast of future conditions.

                 In applying this forward-looking approach, a distinction                 在 應 用 這 種 前 瞻 性 方 法 時， 區 別 在
                 is made between:                                                         於：

                 –     financial instruments that have not deteriorated                   –    自初始確認後信用質量未顯著
                       significantly in credit quality since initial                           惡化或信用風險較低的金融工
                       recognition or that have low credit risk (“Stage 1”);                   具（「第一階段」）；及
                       and

                 –     financial instruments that have deteriorated                       –    自初始確認以來信用質量顯著
                       significantly in credit quality since initial                           惡化且信用風險不低的金融工
                       recognition and whose credit risk is not low                            具（「第二階段」）。
                       (“Stage 2”).

                 “Stage 3” would cover financial assets that have                         「第三階段」將涵蓋在報告期末具有
                 objective evidence of impairment at the end of the                       客觀減值證據的金融資產。
                 reporting period.

                 “12-month ECL” are recognised for the Stage 1 category                   第 一 階 段 類 別 將 確 認「12 個 月 預 期
                 while “lifetime ECL” are recognised for the Stage 2                      信貸虧損」，而第二階段類別則確認
                 category.                                                                「存續期預期信貸虧損」。

                 Measurement of the ECL is determined by a probability-                   預期信貸虧損的計量方法取決於金
                 weighted estimate of credit losses over the expected life                融工具預計使用年限內信貸虧損的
                 of the financial instrument.                                             概率加權估計。

           Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment,                     不 論 上 述 評 估 結 果， 於 合 約 付 款 逾
           the Group presumes that the credit risk has increased                    期超過 30 日時，則本集團假設信貸
           significantly since initial recognition when contractual                 風 險 自 初 步 確 認 以 來 大 幅 增 加， 除

           Group has reasonable and supportable information that                    示並非如此。
           demonstrates otherwise.

           Despite the aforegoing, the Group assumes that the                       儘 管 如 此， 如 果 債 務 工 具 於 各 報 告
           credit risk on a debt instrument has not increased                       日 期 被 釐 定 為 具 低 信 貸 風 險， 則 本
           significantly since initial recognition if the debt                      集團可假設該債務工具的信貸風險
           instrument is determined to have low credit risk at the                  自 初 步 確 認 後 未 有 顯 著 增 加。 假 若
           end of each reporting period. A debt instrument is                       債 務 工 具 的 違 約 風 險 較 低， 借 款 人
           determined to have low credit risk if it has a low risk of               在短期內絕對有能力履行其合約現
           default, the borrower has strong capacity to meet its                    金 流 量 的 義 務， 以 及 經 濟 和 商 業 條
           contractual cash flow obligations in the near term and                   件 長 遠 出 現 不 利 變 化， 可 能 但 不 一
           adverse changes in economic and business conditions                      定會降低借款人履行合約現金流量
           in the longer term may, but will not necessarily, reduce                 義 務 的 能 力， 則 債 務 工 具 被 視 為 具
           the ability of the borrower to fulfill its contractual cash              有 較 低 信 貸 風 險。 本 集 團 認 為， 倘
           flow obligations. The Group considers a financial asset                  一項金融資產擁有按全球公認定義
           to have low credit risk when it has an internal or external              的 內 部 或 外 部「投 資 級 別 」信 用 評
           credit rating of “investment grade” as per globally                      級， 則 該 金 融 資 產 被 視 為 具 有 低 信
           understood definition.                                                   貸風險。

           For internal credit risk management, the Group                           就 內 部 信 貸 風 險 管 理 而 言， 本 集 團
           considers an event of default occurs when there is a                     認為，當 (i) 違約事件在內部制訂或
           breach of financial covenants by the counterparty; or                    得自外界取得的資料顯示債務人不
           when (i) information developed internally or obtained                    大可能悉數向債權人（包括本集團）
           from external sources indicates that the debtor is                       還 款（未 計 及 本 集 團 所 持 任 何 抵 押
           unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full              品）或 (ii) 金融資產逾期 90 日時發生。
           (without taking into account any collateral held by the
           Group) or (ii) the financial asset is 90 days past due.

           Detailed analysis of the ECL assessment of trade                         有關貿易應收款項及其他按攤銷成
           receivables and other financial assets measured at                       本計量的金融資產的預期信貸虧損

                 For multiple contracts, contract assets and contract                   多 份 合 約 而 言， 不 相 關 合 約 的 合 約

     POLICIES – continued
     3.11 Leases                                                           3.11 租賃

         Definition of a lease and the Group as a lessee                      租賃的定義及本集團作為承租人

         At inception of a contract, the Group considers whether              於 合 同 開 始 時， 本 集 團 會 考 慮 合 約
         a contract is, or contains a lease. A lease is defined as            是 否 屬 租 賃 或 包 含 租 賃。 租 賃 定 義
         “a contract, or part of a contract, that conveys the right           為「賦予於一段時間內使用已識別資
         to use an identified asset (the underlying asset) for a              產（相關資產）的權利以換取代價的
         period of time in exchange for consideration”. To apply              一項合約或合約的一部分」。為應用
         this definition, the Group assesses whether the contract             該 定 義， 本 集 團 評 估 有 關 合 約 是 否
         meets three key evaluations which are whether:                       符合三個關鍵評估項，即：

         •     the contract contains an identified asset, which               •      合約中明示或資產可供本集團
               is either explicitly identified in the contract or                    使用時暗示合約是否包括一項
               implicitly specified by being identified at the time                  已識別資產；
               the asset is made available to the Group;

         •     the Group has the right to obtain substantially all            •      經考慮其於合約界定範圍內的
               of the economic benefits from use of the identified                   權利後，本集團是否有權於整
               asset throughout the period of use, considering                       個使用期間因使用已識別資產
               its rights within the defined scope of the contract;                  而獲得絕大部分經濟利益；及
               and

         •     the Group has the right to direct the use of the               •      本集團於整個使用期間是否有
               identified asset throughout the period of use. The                    權指示已識別資產的使用。本
               Group assesses whether it has the right to direct                     集團會評估其於整個使用期間
               ‘how and for what purpose’ the asset is used                          是 否 有 權 指 示 資 產 的「使 用 方
               throughout the period of use.                                         式及用途」。

         For contracts that contains a lease component and one                就包含租賃部分及一項或多項額外
         or more additional lease or non-lease components, the                租 賃 或 非 租 賃 部 分 的 合 約 而 言， 本
         Group allocates the consideration in the contract to                 集團將合約代價分配至各租賃部分
         each lease and non-lease component on the basis of                   及 非 租 賃 部 分， 並 按 該 等 部 分 的 相
         their relative stand-alone prices.                                   對獨立價格為基準進行分配。

                use asset for impairment when such indicator exists.                     集團亦會對使用權資產進行減值評
                                                                                         估。

                (“IBR”).                                                                 進行貼現。

     3.13 Share capital                                                      3.13 股本

          Ordinary shares are classified as equity attributable to              普 通 股 歸 為 權 益 持 有 人 應 佔 權 益。
          the equity holders. The amount of the share capital is                普 通 股 的 股 本 金 額 以 面 值 確 定， 從
          recognised using the nominal value and any related                    股票溢價中扣除任何相關交易成本。
          transaction costs are deducted from the share premium.

     3.14 Revenue recognition                                                3.14 收益確認

          Revenue arises mainly from the sales of jewellery                     收益主要來自銷售珠寶產品。
          products.

          To determine whether to recognise revenue, the Group                  為 釐 定 是 否 確 認 收 益， 本 集 團 遵 循
          follows a 5-step process:                                             五個步驟程序：

          1.    Identifying the contract with a customer                        1.     確立與客戶訂立的合約

          2.    Identifying the performance obligations                         2.     確立履約責任

          3.    Determining the transaction price                               3.     釐定交易價格

          4.    Allocating the transaction price to the performance             4.     將交易價格分配至履約責任
                obligations

          5.    Recognising revenue when/as performance                         5.     當╱於實體完成履約責任時確
                obligation(s) are satisfied                                            認收益

          In all cases, the total transaction price for a contract is           在 所 有 情 況 下， 合 約 的 總 交 易 價 格
          allocated amongst the various performance obligations                 乃按履約責任的相對獨立售價分配
          based on their relative stand-alone selling prices. The               至 各 項 履 約 責 任。 合 約 的 交 易 價 格
          transaction price for a contract excludes any amounts                 不包括代表第三方收取的任何金額。
          collected on behalf of third parties.

          Revenue is recognised either at a point in time or over               當（或於）本集團透過向其客戶轉移
          time, when (or as) the Group satisfies performance                    約 定 商 品 或 服 務 完 成 履 約 責 任 時，

                value cannot be reliably estimated, in which case they                值 未 能 可 靠 計 量 則 除 外， 在 此 情 況
                are measured at the fair value of the equity instruments              下， 則 按 所 授 出 權 益 工 具 之 公 允 價
                granted. In all cases, the fair value is measured at the              值 計 量。 在 所 有 情 況 下， 公 允 價 值
                date the Group obtains the goods or the counterparty                  乃於本集團取得貨品或對手方提供
                renders the services.                                                 服務當日計量。

           incurred for completion and disposal. These estimates are                計乃基於當前市況及銷售性質相若貨品的
           based on the current market condition and the historical                 過往經驗進行。
           experience in selling goods of similar nature.

           any slow-moving inventories and estimated the amount of                  過往銷售類似性質貨品的經驗再估計撥備
           provision primarily based on the pricing policy and strategies           金額。此外，本集團亦委聘獨立專業珠寶
           and the historical experience in selling goods of similar                鑑 定 師 以 進 行 估 值， 並 參 照 國 際 鑽 石 價
           nature. The Group also engaged an independent professional               格、其專業知識及現時市況，對珠寶鑲嵌

     Leases – Estimation of IBR                                               租賃 – 估算增量借款利率

     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in         本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
     a lease, therefore, it uses its IBR to measure lease liabilities.        率，因此，其使用增量借款利率計量租賃
     The IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would have to             負債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似經濟
     pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security,          環境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之資
     the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to the         產，而以類似抵押品於類似期間借入所需
     right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment. The IBR            資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率
     therefore reflects what the Group “would have to pay”, which             反映了本集團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀
     requires estimation when no observable rates are available               察的利率時（例如當附屬公司沒有進行融資
     (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into financing               交易時）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租賃
     transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect the             的條款及條件時（例如租賃並非以附屬公司
     terms and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases              的功能貨幣進行時），則須作出利率估計。
     are not in the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group              當可觀察輸入數據可用時，本集團使用可
     estimates the IBR using observable inputs (such as market                觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）估算增量借款
     interest rates) when available and is required to make certain           利率並須作出若干實體特定估計（如附屬公
     entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone          司的獨立信貸評級）。
     credit rating).

     Estimation of impairment of trade and other receivables                  於香港財務報告準則第 9 號預期信貸虧損
     within the scope of ECL under HKFRS 9                                    範圍內的貿易及其他應收款項的減值估計

     The Group makes allowances on items subjects to ECL                      本集團基於有關違約風險及預期虧損率的
     (including trade and other receivables) based on assumptions             假設就受限於預期信貸虧損的項目（包括貿
     about risk of default and expected loss rates. The Group uses            易及其他應收款項）計提撥備。誠如附註
     judgment in making these assumptions and selecting the                   3.7 所載，本集團於作出該等假設及選擇計

26. LSP OBLIGATIONS                                                             26. 長期服務金責任
    Pursuant to the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance, Chapter                              （香港法例第 57 章），在某
                                                                                  根據《僱傭條例》
    57, Hong Kong employees that have been employed                               些情況下（如被僱主解僱或退休），連續受
    continuously for at least five years are entitled to LSP under                聘至少五年的香港僱員有權領取長期服務
    certain circumstances (e.g. dismissal by employers or upon                    金。
    retirement).

    The amount of LSP payable is determined with reference to                     應付長期服務金金額乃參考僱員的最後月
    the employee’s last monthly salary (capped at HK$22,500)                      薪（上 限 為 22,500 港 元 ）及 服 務 年 期 而 釐
    and the years of service, reduced by the amount of any                        定，並扣除本集團向強積金計劃供款所產
    accrued benefits derived from the Group’s contributions to                    生的任何累算權益金額（見附註 9），每名
    MPF scheme (see note 9), with an overall cap of HK$390,000                    僱員的整體上限為 390,000 港元。目前，
    per employee. Currently, the Group does not have any                          本集團並無任何單獨的資金安排以履行其
    separate funding arrangement in place to meet its LSP                         長期服務金責任。
    obligations.

    I n J u n e 2022, t h e H o n g K o n g S A R G o v e r n m e n t ( t h e     於 2022 年 6 月，香港特別行政區政府（「政
    “Government”) gazetted the Hong Kong Employment and                           府 」）在 憲 報 刊 登《2022 年 僱 傭 及 退 休 計
    Retirement Schemes Legislation (Offsetting Arrangement)                       劃 法 例（抵 銷 安 排 ）
                                                                                                （修 訂 ）條 例 》
                                                                                                          （「修 訂 條
    (Amendment) Ordinance 2022 (the “Amendment Ordinance”),                       例」），取消使用僱主強制強積金供款所產
    which abolishes the use of the accrued benefits derived from                  生的累算權益來對沖長期服務金的規定。
    employers’ mandatory MPF contributions to offset the LSP.                     修訂條例將於 2025 年 5 月 1 日（簡稱「過渡
    The Amendment Ordinance took effect on 1 May 2025 (the                        日」）生效。此外，政府已表示將在取消對
    “Transition Date”). Separately, the Government implemented                    沖安排後推出補貼計劃，以協助僱主。
    a scheme to subsidise a portion of the post-transition LSP
    payable by an employer up to a certain amount per employee
    per year for a period of 25 years after the Transition Date (the
    “LSP Subsidy”).

    Among other things, upon the abolition of the offsetting                      其中，一旦取消抵銷機制生效，僱主便不
    mechanism takes effect, an employer can no longer use                         能再以強制性強積金供款所產生的任何累
    any of the accrued benefits derived from its mandatory                        算權益（不論是在過渡日期之前、當日或之
    MPF contributions (irrespective of the contributions made                     後所作的供款），來扣減僱員由過渡日期起
    before, on or after the Transition Date) to reduce the LSP in                 的服務年期的長期服務金。然而，倘僱員
    respect of an employee’s service from the Transition Date.                    於過渡日期前已開始受僱，則僱主可繼續
    However, where an employee’s employment commenced                             以上述累算權益扣減僱員在過渡日期前的
    before the Transition Date, the employer can continue to use                  服務年期的長期服務金。此外，有關過渡
    the above accrued benefits to reduce the LSP in respect                       日期前的服務的長期服務金將根據僱員在
    of the employee’s service up to that date. In addition, the                   緊接過渡日期前的月薪及截至該日期的服
    LSP in respect of the service before the Transition Date                      務年期計算。
    will be calculated based on the employee’s monthly salary

           Date.

           its abolition as disclosed in note 3.17 to the consolidated       入 賬， 並 於 綜 合 財 務 報 表 附 註 3.17 中 披
           financial statements.                                             露。

           primarily impacts the Group’s LSP obligations with respect to     香港僱員的長期服務金責任。
           Hong Kong employees.

           year ended 31 March 2026.                                         並無權獲得任何長期服務金資助。

31. RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEMES                                        31. 退休福利計劃
    The Group operates MPF Scheme for all qualifying employees          本集團為所有香港合資格僱員設立強制性
    in Hong Kong. The assets of the scheme are held separately          公積金計劃。計劃的資產與本集團的資產
    from those of the Group, in funds under the control of              分開處理，並由受託人管理的基金持有。
    trustees. The Group contributes 5% of relevant payroll costs        本集團向該計劃作出的供款為有關薪金
    to the scheme, which contribution is matched by employees           成 本 的 5%， 僱 員 須 作 等 額 供 款， 惟 每 名
    but subject to a maximum amount of HK$1,500 per month for           僱員每月向該計劃供款最高金額不超過
    each employee to the scheme.                                        1,500 港元。

    The employees employed by the PRC subsidiary are members            由中國附屬公司僱傭的僱員為中國政府營
    of the state-managed retirement benefits schemes operated           辦的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員。中國
    by the PRC government. The PRC subsidiary is required               附屬公司須按其薪金的若干百分比向退休
    to contribute a certain percentage of their payroll to the          福 利 計 劃 作 出 供 款 以 為 福 利 提 供 資 金。
    retirement benefit schemes to fund the benefits. The only           本集團對於該等退休福利計劃的唯一責任
    obligation of the Group with respect to the retirement benefit      為根據計劃作出所需供款。
    schemes is to make the required contributions under the
    schemes.

    retirement benefit schemes in future years.                         計劃之應付供款。

      33. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                            33. 資本風險管理
           The Group manages its capital to ensure that the Group will          本集團管理其資本以確保本集團將能夠繼
           be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the          續持續經營，同時透過優化債務及權益結
           return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt          餘以盡量提高股東回報。本集團整體策略
           and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains             於該兩個年度內保持不變。
           unchanged throughout the year.

           The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which          本集團資本架構包括債務（包括附註 24 及
           includes lease liabilities and bank borrowings as set out            25 分別所載租賃負債及銀行借款）及本集
           in notes 24 and 25, respectively and equity attributable             團擁有人應佔權益（包括綜合財務報表所披
           to owners of the Company, comprising share capital and               露的股本及儲備）。
           reserves as disclosed in the consolidated financial statements.



           The directors of the Company review the capital structure            本公司董事定期檢討資本架構。本集團考
           regularly. The Group considers the cost of capital and the           慮資本成本以及各類資本相關風險，並將
           risks associated with each class of capital, and will balance        會透過新股份發行以及籌措或償還銀行借
           its overall capital structure through new share issues and           款平衡其整體資本架構。
           raising or repayment of bank borrowings.

           continued

           Fair value measurements                                                   公平值計量

           Financial assets measured at fair value in the consolidated               於綜合財務狀況表中按公平值計量的金融
           statement of financial position are grouped into three levels of          資產可分為三個公平值層級。三個層級基
           a fair value hierarchy. The three levels are defined based on             於可觀察之重大輸入值，定義如下：
           the observability of significant inputs to the measurements, as
           follows:

           Level 1:    quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for              第一級： 相同資產及負債於活躍市場之報
                       identical assets and liabilities                                   價（未經調整）

           Level 2:    inputs other than quoted prices included within               第二級： 就資產或負債而直接或間接可觀
                       Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability,            察及並無使用重大不可觀察輸入
                       either directly or indirectly, and not using significant           參 數 之 輸 入 數 據（第 一 級 內 包 括
                       unobservable inputs                                                的報價除外）。

           Level 3:    significant unobservable inputs for the asset or              第三級： 輸入數據為資產或負債的重大無
                       liability                                                          法觀察輸入數據。

           The level in the fair value hierarchy within which the financial          各項金融資產整體所應歸入的公平值架構
           asset is categorised in its entirety is based on the lowest level         內的層級，應基於對公平值計量具有重大
           of input that is significant to the fair value measurement.               意義的最低層級資料輸入值。

37. EVENT AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                  37. 報告期後的事項
    In May 2026, Depasser Group Holdings Company Limited (a             於 2026 年 5 月， 本 公 司 其 中 一 家 直 接 全
    directly wholly-owned subsidiary) (“Depasser”), entered into        資 附 屬 公 司 Depasser Group Holdings
    a sale and purchase agreement with Mr. Fu, who held 76%             Company Limited（「Depasser」）與 傅 先
    equity interests of Dia Myth approximately, an associate of the     生訂立買賣協議，傅先生持有本集團聯營
    Group, pursuant to which Depasser has agreed to acquire,            公 司 卓 薈 之 76% 權 益。 據 此，Depasser
    and Mr. Fu has agreed to sell 25% equity interests of Dia           已 同 意 收 購， 而 傅 先 生 已 同 意 出 售 卓 薈
    Myth for a cash consideration of approximately HK$8.8 million       25% 股 權， 現 金 代 價 約 為 8.8 百 萬 港 元
    (the “Acquisition”). Upon completion of the transaction, the        （「該收購」）。該交易完成後，本集團將間
    Group indirectly holds 45% equity interests in Dia Myth, which      接持有卓薈之 45% 股權，卓薈仍為本集團
    remains the associate of the Group. The Acquisition has been        之 聯 營 公 司。 該 收 購 已 於 2026 年 5 月 完
    completed in May 2026. Details of the Acquisition were set out      成。有關進一步詳情，請參閱本公司日期
    in the Company’s announcement dated 13 May 2026.                    為 2026 年 5 月 13 日之公告。
